Appendix

Appendix A
Formulating the global modes for Kasner worldsheet expansion

In this section, we outline the key steps to formulate the global (or Unruh-Minkowski) modes for the Kasner worldsheet quantum mode expansion (
3.4
). We begin by setting up the right-moving sector of the positive-frequency global Kasner modes. Utilizing the coordinate mapping (
3.3
), we successively reexpress the local Kasner modes
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}
,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.16806pt{\rm P}\kern 3.675pt{*}}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.11806pt{\rm P}\kern 2.625pt{*}}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}
,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}
, and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.07085pt{\rm F}\kern 3.675pt{*}}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.02084pt{\rm F}\kern 2.625pt{*}}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}
successively as follows:

\displaystyle\sqrt{4\pi}n\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle=i\left(e^{\eta^{-}_{\rm F}a/c}\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a}=ie^{-i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left[a\left(\sigma^{-}-\varphi\right)/c\right]^{-i\Omega_{n}/a},

(113a)

\displaystyle\sqrt{4\pi}n\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.16806pt{\rm P}\kern 3.675pt{*}}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.11806pt{\rm P}\kern 2.625pt{*}}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle=-i\left(e^{-\eta^{+}_{\rm P}a/c}\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a}=-i\left(-1\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a}e^{-i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left[a\left(\sigma^{-}-\varphi\right)/c\right]^{-i\Omega_{n}/a},

(113b)

\displaystyle\sqrt{4\pi}n\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle=i\left(e^{-\eta^{-}_{\rm P}a/c}\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}=ie^{i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left[a\left(-\sigma^{+}-\varphi\right)/c\right]^{i\Omega_{n}/a},

(113c)

\displaystyle\sqrt{4\pi}n\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.07085pt{\rm F}\kern 3.675pt{*}}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.02084pt{\rm F}\kern 2.625pt{*}}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle=-i\left(e^{\eta^{+}_{\rm F}a/c}\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}=-i\left(-1\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}e^{i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left[a\left(-\sigma^{+}-\varphi\right)/c\right]^{i\Omega_{n}/a}.

(113d)

We then obtain the following mode combinations:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}-e^{-\pi\Omega_{n}/a}\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.16806pt{\rm P}\kern 3.675pt{*}}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.11806pt{\rm P}\kern 2.625pt{*}}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle={i\over\sqrt{\pi}n}\left(a/c\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a}e^{-i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left(c\tau-\sigma-\varphi\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a},

(114)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}-e^{-\pi\Omega_{n}/a}\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.07085pt{\rm F}\kern 3.675pt{*}}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.02084pt{\rm F}\kern 2.625pt{*}}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle={i\over\sqrt{\pi}n}\left(a/c\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}e^{i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left(-c\tau-\sigma-\varphi\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a},

(115)

which are well-defined and analytically continued between the F and P wedges for the right-moving global Kasner modes in Minkowski coordinates. Note that in steps (
113b
) and (
113d
) (corresponding to the negative frequency left-moving modes in P and F, respectively), there is an apparent ambiguity in the choice of
\left(-1\right)^{\mp i\Omega_{n}/a}:-1=e^{i\pi}
or
-1=e^{-i\pi}
. However, it is crucial to note that
\sigma^{-}>0
(
<0
) and
\sigma^{+}>0
(
<0
) in F (P). Thus, to maintain positive frequencies for the right-moving global mode combinations (
114
) and (
115
), we must satisfy
\operatorname{Im}\,\sigma^{-}>0
in step (
113b
) (continuing from P to F) and
\operatorname{Im}\,\sigma^{-}<0
in step (
113d
) (continuing from F to P). In other words, the global mode (
114
) is analytic in the upper half of the complex
\sigma^{-}
plane, allowing us to set
-1=e^{i\pi}
in step (
113b
). Similarly, we set
-1=e^{-i\pi}
in step (
113d
), as the global mode (
115
) is analytic in the lower-half complex
\sigma^{-}
plane.

Next, we turn to the setup for left-moving sector of the positive-frequency global Kasner modes. Using the light-cone coordinate mapping (
3.3
), we can successively restructure the local Kasner modes
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.07085pt{\rm F}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.02084pt{\rm F}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}
,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}
,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.16806pt{\rm P}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.11806pt{\rm P}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}
, and
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}
as follows:

\displaystyle\sqrt{4\pi}n\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.07085pt{\rm F}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.02084pt{\rm F}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle=i\left(e^{\eta^{+}_{\rm F}a/c}\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a}=ie^{-i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left[a\left(\sigma^{+}+\varphi\right)/c\right]^{-i\Omega_{n}/a},

(116a)

\displaystyle\sqrt{4\pi}n\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle=-i\left(e^{-\eta^{-}_{\rm P}a/c}\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a}=-i\left(-1\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a}e^{-i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left[a\left(\sigma^{+}+\varphi\right)/c\right]^{-i\Omega_{n}/a},

(116b)

\displaystyle\sqrt{4\pi}n\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.16806pt{\rm P}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.11806pt{\rm P}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle=i\left(e^{-\eta^{+}_{\rm P}a/c}\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}=ie^{i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left[a\left(-\sigma^{-}+\varphi\right)/c\right]^{i\Omega_{n}/a},

(116c)

\displaystyle\sqrt{4\pi}n\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle=-i\left(e^{\eta^{+}_{\rm F}a/c}\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}=-i\left(-1\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}e^{i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left[a\left(-\sigma^{-}+\varphi\right)/c\right]^{i\Omega_{n}/a}.

(116d)

In a similar manner, we derive the following mode combinations, analytically continued between the F and P wedges (i.e., along the
\sigma=0
surface) to produce the left-moving global Kasner modes:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.21255pt{\rm F}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.07085pt{\rm F}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.02084pt{\rm F}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}-e^{-\pi\Omega_{n}/a}\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.0014pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.04863pt{\rm P}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle={i\over\sqrt{\pi}n}\left(a/c\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a}e^{-i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left(c\tau+\sigma+\varphi\right)^{-i\Omega_{n}/a},

(117)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-9.34865pt{\rm P}\kern 5.90282pt}}_{{\kern-5.51393pt\kern 5.90282pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-6.16806pt{\rm P}\kern 3.675pt}}_{{\kern-3.28612pt\kern 3.675pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm P}}}}{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-5.11806pt{\rm P}\kern 2.625pt}}_{{\kern-2.23611pt\kern 2.625pt{n}}}}-e^{-\pi\Omega_{n}/a}\,\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-8.86531pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}{\hphantom{{}^{{{\rm F}}}}\tilde{\mathcal{U}}^{{\kern-7.95142pt{\rm F}\kern 5.55557pt{*}}}_{{\kern-5.16669pt\kern 5.55557pt{n}}}}

\displaystyle={i\over\sqrt{\pi}n}\left(a/c\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}e^{i\Omega_{n}\psi}\left(-c\tau+\sigma+\varphi\right)^{i\Omega_{n}/a}.

(118)

As before, we encounter the ambiguity in choosing
\left(-1\right)^{\mp i\Omega_{n}/a}
in steps (
116b
) and (
116d
), which involve the negative frequency right-moving modes in the P and F regions, respectively. To ensure positive frequencies for the left-moving global mode combinations (
117
) and (
118
), we require
\operatorname{Im},\sigma^{+}>0
in step (
116b
) (continuing from P to F) and
\operatorname{Im},\sigma^{+}<0
in step (
116d
) (continuing from F to P). Consequently, we set
-1=e^{i\pi}
in step (
116b
) and
-1=e^{-i\pi}
in step (
116d
).

It is important to note that all the forms of the global Kasner worldsheet modes derived in
eqs.

114
,
115
,
117
and
118
are unnormalized. To properly normalize these right- and left-moving global Kasner modes in the F and P regions, we need to adjust them to become orthonormal by determining the appropriate normalization constants. This process will yield the normalized forms of the global modes and their Hermitian conjugates for the Kasner worldsheet, as presented in
eqs.

61
and
65
.
